1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the elementary object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 313. The moduli space has dimension at most 284 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the elementary decomposition. By minimality, the element a^313 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 284-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
